######## THE A/B's REPORT, VERDICT AND TABLES

==== s53/r5/ab/AB-REPORT.md (sha256 cb8ee823dbc04d6f728f7ea90aed69ae7bf41ab129b947df4aa2ac26522e542f, 26862 bytes; LF text sha256 cb8ee823dbc04d6f728f7ea90aed69ae7bf41ab129b947df4aa2ac26522e542f) ====
# R5 live A/B: a free screen before "done" (held-out seeds; 2026-10-28, 10:00 PM to 10:20 PM Los Angeles time)

## 0. Verdict (the caveat is in it)

**PASS.** `SUMMARY.txt` reads: "VERDICT: PASS: 4 of 4 seeds counted (floor 3): OLD ended done NOT free and NEW done FREE with
the exact reason on every counted seed; the PF-17 control passed".

- **All 4 held-out seeds (5351-5354) reached the state in both arms**, at the same loop top: the one free frame after DAD's
  warp to PETALBURG CITY. The two arms' loop-top save states are byte-identical for every seed.
- **OLD (R5 with G347) ended "done" right there, NOT held free.** On the next frame the script status is 0 and the controls
  are locked (`extra.txt`). A tapped direction was not taken.
  - By the decomp, that is the city's ON_FRAME script, WALLY's tutorial.
  - From the byte-identical state, every NEW run then played that tutorial's battle: WALLY's Zigzagoon L7 against a RALTS,
    starting 1,449 to 1,509 frames after the loop top (`tutorial_check.txt`).
- **NEW (R5) played WALLY's tutorial and DAD's speech out** (11,006 to 11,130 frames). It ended held free in the gym, with
  input taken.
- **Both arms' reasons are exactly** `done: ch2_dad reached` (the play loop's) and `play: done: ch2_dad reached` (the
  harness's).
- **The PF-17 control (seed 5355, `ch1_pokedex`) passed:** "done" free, nothing played out (`settled` False, a 44-frame
  pause).
- **CAVEAT 1: what discriminated is the settle's decision PAUSE** (the builder's deviation 1, mutant G349), not its
  `not nav_free` play-out branch.
  - On all 4 loop tops the AI's own `nav_free` read the screen FREE: `ai_nav_free_on_its_look: True` in every NEW run
    (`extra.txt`).
  - So, by the settle's code, its first step there was the pause. The two play-outs per run came after it.
  - No arm with the pause removed was run. I make no claim about what that variant would do.
- **CAVEAT 2: R5's own end record cannot see this case.** `report.end_screen.free` read True on all 4 OLD ends, which the
  game held NOT free. It is the AI's `nav_free` (D5: "a guard, never evidence").
- **CAVEAT 3: the arms are not independent samples within a seed.** Up to the decision, each seed is ONE trajectory shared
  by both arms (byte-identical loop-top states; CLAUDE.md rule 8). The 4 seeds are 4 trajectories, all the same mechanism.

Every number below is copied from a saved file in `s53/r5/ab/` (this folder), and each one is named.

- **The verdict** is `SUMMARY.txt`: the registered grader `ab_summary.py`, run on the saved `PROBE` lines of
  `runs/*/probe.out`.
- **The markdown table in section 3** is `table.md`, printed by `ab_table.py` with that grader's own functions.

## 1. What was run

- **Worktree under test.** `C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement/.claude/worktrees/wf_ab039e6b-79e-1`, HEAD
  `714084b0`, R5 uncommitted. Its working-tree digest is `29a5dc81286162ff` (the `code:` line in `ledger_begin_ab.out`),
  the build report's final tree.
- **The two arms** (`make_trees.out`, exit 0, "TREES OK"). Both trees were made by `revert_step5.make_copy` itself (imported
  from the worktree), with `tempfile.tempdir` pointed at `ab/trees/`, so the copies never touched the worktree.
  - **OLD** = `trees/old_G347/`: the R5 `ai_player/` with mutant G347's one edit, exactly as `revert_step5.py` applies it
    (the anchor must occur once). The settle's call,
    `yield from self._settle_before_done(hands, nav, look, options.target, report)`, becomes `pass  # MUTANT G347`.
  - **NEW** = `trees/new_R5/`: the same copy with no edit (revert_step5's G0).
  - OLD and NEW differ in `mode.py` only, by that one line. NEW is identical, file by file, to the worktree's
    `ai_player/` (168 files; the copy's own ignores, `__pycache__`, `evidence`, `*.ss1`, aside).
  - OLD keeps the rest of R5, including `_end_screen` (D5's end record, a read: no frame passes). So the OLD arm still
    writes `report.end_screen`.
- **The fixture.** `AI_PLAYER_TARGET=ch2_dad` from a REAL New Game (no state: the AI plays the intro itself), the builder's
  natural fixture. In-process through `play_harness.play`, like the builder's probe. The cartridge clock is pinned to
  `rate_step5.GAME_CLOCK` 2026-10-14T10:00 and the wall clock is `WALL_CLOCK` 10:00. The personality and starter are
  `rate_step5.plan_of(seed)`. The frame budget is 400,000 for both arms (the builder used 300,000; his two runs ended at
  199,479 and 251,736 frames, so 400,000 gives room for a slower personality plus NEW's settle).
- **The discriminator.** The builder's own harness-side free read, `probe_end_screen.py`, copied here verbatim (sha256
  `d5c6095b…` in both places, `free_read_copy.sha256`). FREE means `gMain.callback2 == CB2_Overworld`, script context
  status 2, `sLockFieldControls` 0, `sFieldMessageBoxMode` 0 and text printer 0 inactive. **`free_held`** means free on
  the end frame AND on 10 more frames run with no input. The behavioural input check (a tapped direction turns the
  player in a throwaway copy of the frame) and the AI's own `nav_free` are reported beside it, never instead of it (PF-19).
- **The reach instrument** (PF-4; CLAUDE.md rule 7, "rebind the callee to capture it"). The driver `ab_probe.py` is the
  builder's `probe_end_screen.run` plus one recording-only wrapper.
  - Before `play` starts, the harness rebinds `HandsBrain._play_end` and `HandsBrain._settle_before_done` in the arm's
    tree. Each wrapper records, then calls the original unchanged with the same arguments.
  - The FIRST call for a "done" end is the loop top where the run decided "done". In NEW it is `_settle_before_done`; in
    OLD it is `_play_end`, because G347 deleted the settle's call. No frame passes between the pass's look and either
    call.
  - There the wrapper records the frame and takes one save state (a read: no frame runs).
  - After the run, the harness loads that state and reads it exactly as it reads the end: the game's proof of the target
    (`story_truth`), the input check and `free_held`.
- **The criteria, written before any held-out seed was played** (the `#45`/`#46` notes, and `ab_summary.py`, whose sha256
  is in the registered numbers):
  - **REACH**, per arm: at that loop top the game's proof of ch2_dad (`HIDE_PETALBURG_CITY_WALLYS_MOM`) holds, and
    `free_held` is False. A seed COUNTS only if both arms reached it at the same loop-top frame.
  - **REACH FLOOR:** 3 counted seeds of the 4. Zero counted is VACUOUS; one or two is INCOMPLETE.
  - **OLD** on a counted seed: the play loop's own end string exactly `done: ch2_dad reached`, the harness's reason exactly
    `play: done: ch2_dad reached` (the string `live_step5.py:350` compares), and the END not held free.
  - **NEW** on a counted seed: the same two exact strings, and the END held free.
  - **PF-17 CONTROL** (NEW, `ch1_pokedex`, its own held-out seed, 300,000 frames): the two exact strings, the END held
    free, and the settle played nothing out (`report.settle.played` 0, `report.end_screen.settled` False).
  - **FAIL** if any counted seed or the control misses its criteria. Otherwise VACUOUS or INCOMPLETE by the floor (or a
    missing control), else PASS.

## 2. The instrument check, before any held-out seed (a REPLAY, ledger #44)

The driver and the grader were checked on development seed 5301 first. Both arms were started from the builder's saved
`ch2_dad_5301` end state and world memory, which is the builder's `devab` design (`hookcheck/*/probe.out`).

| Arm | Loop top (wrapper, pad frame, emulator frame) | Loop-top state md5 | Loop-top `free_held` | End | End `free_held` | Input taken at end | Frames |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| OLD | `_play_end`, 0, 251736 | `149f4d37160fbf55…` (= its end state) | False (+1: status 0, lock 1) | `done: ch2_dad reached` | False | no | 0 |
| NEW | `_settle_before_done`, 0, 251736 (look frame 251736) | `149f4d37160fbf55…` | False (+1: status 0, lock 1) | `done: ch2_dad reached` | True | yes | 10,997 |

- **NEW reproduced the builder's development numbers exactly:** 10,997 frames, settle played 2, paused 112 (the
  `new_5301` row of `build/probe/SUMMARY.txt`). So the wrapper did not move the trajectory. This is one trajectory
  replayed, a determinism check (CLAUDE.md rule 8).
- **The AI's own `nav_free` read that not-free loop top as FREE** (`ai_nav_free_on_its_look: true` in NEW's wrapper
  record). That is the PF-19 trap the builder measured, and the reason the settle needed its pause (deviation 1).
- **The grader can fail and pass** (`selftest.out`: "SELFTEST VERDICT: PASS (12 of 12 cases)", exit 0). On these two
  real runs, each arm's criteria pass on its own arm and FAIL on the other arm's run. The control's criteria FAIL on the
  `ch2_dad` run. Four synthetic edits also fail: a loop top held free, a missing proof, another end string, and a
  near-miss reason string.

  read by the harness after the run, for the record. "Frames" is the harness's count of frames run. For the control,
  "Reached False" means its loop top was itself held free: the normal case, which a control should be.
- **Reach and determinism** (`SUMMARY.txt`, `extra.txt`).
  - The loop-top state md5s are identical across the arms: 5351 `a3c2c634…`, 5352 `a079c1d6…`, 5353 `a6f79ca6…`, 5354
    `7e4d7c17…`.
  - OLD's end state is byte-identical to its loop-top state ("same bytes as the end state: True"), and its frames equal
    the loop-top pad frame: no frame passed between the decision and the end.
  - NEW's frames are the loop top plus exactly the settle's frames: 237,600 − 226,470 = 11,130; 263,212 − 252,159 =
    11,053; 207,374 − 196,357 = 11,017; 320,768 − 309,762 = 11,006.
- **Where they ended** (`end_marks.txt`, read by the harness's own `play_harness.py truth` on the saved states).
  - Every loop top, and so every OLD end, is PETALBURG CITY (map 0,0) at (15, 9), the warp's exit, with
    `HIDE_PETALBURG_CITY_WALLYS_MOM` 1 and `VAR_PETALBURG_GYM_STATE` 1.
  - Every NEW end is the gym (map 8,1) at (4, 108), with `VAR_PETALBURG_GYM_STATE` **2**. That is ch2_wally's proof (≥ 2,
    `milestones.json`).
  - So with R5, a `ch2_dad` run ends with the game past ch2_wally, under the reason `done: ch2_dad reached`: the builder's
    open question 4, now seen on held-out seeds.
  - My first read of these marks used the wrong key names and read None everywhere. It is kept as
    `end_marks_first_try_wrong_keys.txt`; `end_marks.txt` is the corrected read.
- **The game fact** (`decomp_excerpt.txt`, pinned decomp `731ad5b`).
  - The gym script sets `FLAG_HIDE_PETALBURG_CITY_WALLYS_MOM`, `VAR_PETALBURG_GYM_STATE` 1 and `VAR_PETALBURG_CITY_STATE`
    2, then warps to the city at (15, 8).
  - The city's ON_FRAME table runs `PetalburgCity_EventScript_WallyTutorial` (`lockall`) when the city state is 2.
  - The gym's ON_FRAME table runs only at gym state 1, so a NEW end at gym state 2 has no pending gym scene.
- **The behavioural check agrees with the free read in all 9 runs:** not taken at every not-held-free end, and taken at
  every held-free end.
- **The cost of the settle** (deviation 1's pause included): 11,006 to 11,130 frames at `ch2_dad` (the tutorial and the
  speech); 44 frames on the control's free end.

- **Order:**
  1. `#44` (replay, 5301) was begun before the instrument check (`ledger_hookcheck_begin.out`) and finished after it
     (`ledger_hookcheck_finish.out`).
  2. `#45` and `#46` were REGISTERED (graded, `ledger_register_ab.out`, `ledger_register_control.out`) at 10:05:31 PM.
  3. They were BEGUN at 10:05:52 PM (`ledger_begin_ab.out`, `ledger_begin_control.out`). Each begin line reads "this
     run's bounds sha256 … (the same)".
  4. The 9 runs were launched at 10:05:52 PM (`runs/LAUNCH.txt`).
- **The registered numbers' sha256** covers `ab_probe.py`, `probe_end_screen.py`, `ab_summary.py` and `run_ab.sh`
  (`frozen_files.sha256`), both trees' digests, the G347 edit, the target, the budget, the clocks and the criteria. A
  change to any of them after registration would have been refused at `begin`.
- **Held-out check.**
  - Before registering, I read only the NEWEST entries of each ledger: the main checkout's and the three worktrees'.
  - The ledger's own `register`/`begin` checked the worktree's whole ledger: "no other entry of family 'story' names any
    of these seeds" (`ledger_begin_ab.out`).

==== s53/r5/ab/ab_probe.py (sha256 ea1928a306966ac7dcb233c24f8664f1269f9c8044fd1a0c760a37d5e5a7563a, 10587 bytes; LF text sha256 ea1928a306966ac7dcb233c24f8664f1269f9c8044fd1a0c760a37d5e5a7563a) ====
"""R5 LIVE A/B DRIVER (TEST SIDE; never the AI's code): ONE run of the AI's play brain on one arm's frozen tree, with the
GAME's screen read at the loop top where the run decided "done" (THE REACH) and at the END.

    py -3.12 ab_probe.py run --root TREE --seed N --target T --frames F --out DIR [--after K] [--state S --world-in W]

It is the builder's `probe_end_screen.run` (s53/r5/build/probe/; copied here verbatim, sha256 d5c6095b58c6e345...): the
same boot, the cartridge clock pinned to `rate_step5.GAME_CLOCK`, the wall clock `WALL_CLOCK`, the personality and starter
of `rate_step5.plan_of(seed)`, one `play_harness.play` call from a REAL New Game (no `--state`), and the same END reads
(the harness's own free read `game_screen` on the frame the brain returned and on `--after` more frames with no input,
`free_held`; the behavioural input check `accepts_input`; the AI's own `nav_free` FOR THE RECORD ONLY, PF-19), PLUS ONE
recording-only instrument:

THE REACH (LESSONS PF-4; CLAUDE.md rule 7, "instrument the action ... rebind the callee to capture it"). Before `play`
starts, the harness rebinds `HandsBrain._play_end` and `HandsBrain._settle_before_done` (the tree's own methods) with
wrappers that RECORD and then call the original, unchanged, with the same arguments, returning its result. The FIRST of
them called for a "done" end is the loop top where the run decided "done" (`mode.py`: the pass's look, the target's proof
read on it, then the settle (NEW) or nothing (OLD, G347: `pass`), then `_play_end`; no frame passes between the look and
either call). There the wrapper records the frame and takes ONE save state (`emulator.get_save_state()`, a read: no frame
runs, no input changes). AFTER the run, the harness loads that state and reads it exactly as it reads the end: the game's
proof of the target (`play_harness.story_truth`), `accepts_input` and `settled`. Every later wrapper call is recorded
(where, frame) without a state. Nothing in the AI's code is edited: the tree under test is the arm's frozen copy.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import probe_end_screen as pes  # noqa: E402  (the builder's free read, verbatim)


def proof_names(root: Path, target: str) -> dict:
    """The target's proof from the TREE's own milestone table: {mark name: the value it must reach (flags 1)}."""
    data = json.loads((root / "ai_player" / "knowledge" / "milestones.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    milestones = data["milestones"] if isinstance(data, dict) and "milestones" in data else data
    for milestone in milestones:
        if milestone.get("id") == target:
            done = milestone.get("done") or {}
            need = {name: 1 for name in (done.get("flags") or {})}
            for name, spec in (done.get("variables") or {}).items():
                need[name] = int(spec.get("at_least", 1)) if isinstance(spec, dict) else int(spec)
            return need
    raise SystemExit(f"no milestone {target!r} in the tree's milestones.json")


def proof_read(marks: dict, need: dict) -> dict:
    values = {name: marks.get(name) for name in need}
    holds = all(values[name] is not None and values[name] >= need[name] for name in need)
    return {"values": values, "holds": holds}


def run(args) -> dict:
    root = args.root.resolve()
    context = pes.boot_at(root)
    from ai_player import mode as mode_module
    from ai_player.brain.navigator import nav_free
    from ai_player.gates.hands_harness import pin_game_clock, truth
    from ai_player.gates.play_harness import StoryRecorder, play, story_truth
    from ai_player.gates.rate_step5 import GAME_CLOCK, WALL_CLOCK, plan_of
    from ai_player.host.eyes import FrameworkEyes

    loaded = sorted(str(Path(m.__file__).resolve()) for name, m in list(sys.modules.items())
                    if name.split(".")[0] == "ai_player" and getattr(m, "__file__", None))
    outside = [f for f in loaded if not Path(f).is_relative_to(root)]
    if outside:
        raise SystemExit(f"ai_player modules loaded from outside the arm's tree {root}: {outside[:5]}")
    need = proof_names(root, args.target)
    emulator = context.emulator
    if args.state is not None:
        emulator.load_save_state(args.state.read_bytes())
    pin_game_clock(emulator, GAME_CLOCK)
    personality, starter = plan_of(args.seed)
    args.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    run_dir = args.out / "run"
    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ---- THE REACH instrument (module docstring): record, then call the original unchanged
    hook: dict = {"first": None, "calls": [], "play_end": None}
    states: dict = {}
    original_end = mode_module.HandsBrain.__dict__["_play_end"]
    original_settle = mode_module.HandsBrain.__dict__.get("_settle_before_done")

    def record(where: str, hands, look=None) -> None:
        row = {"where": where, "emulator_frame": emulator.get_frame_count(), "pad_frames": hands.pad.frames,
               "look_frame": getattr(look, "frame", None),
               "ai_nav_free_on_its_look": (nav_free(look) if look is not None else None),
               "held_inputs": getattr(emulator, "_held_inputs", None)}
        hook["calls"].append(row)
        if hook["first"] is None:
            hook["first"] = row
            states["reach"] = emulator.get_save_state()

    def play_end(self, hands, story, manager, runner, report, kind, why):
        if kind == "done":
            record("_play_end", hands)
        returned = original_end(self, hands, story, manager, runner, report, kind, why)
        hook["play_end"] = {"kind": kind, "why": why, "returned": returned, "emulator_frame": emulator.get_frame_count(),
                            "pad_frames": hands.pad.frames}
        return returned

    mode_module.HandsBrain._play_end = play_end
    if original_settle is not None:
        def settle(self, hands, nav, look, target, report):
            record("_settle_before_done", hands, look)
            return original_settle(self, hands, nav, look, target, report)

        mode_module.HandsBrain._settle_before_done = settle
    hook["wrapped"] = ["_play_end"] + (["_settle_before_done"] if original_settle is not None else [])

    recorder = StoryRecorder(context, 30, None)
    began = time.monotonic()
    data = play(context, args.seed, args.frames, run_dir, personality, None if args.state else starter, args.target, set(),
                WALL_CLOCK, str(args.world_in) if args.world_in else None, recorder)
    # the brain returned: `play`'s loop breaks on StopIteration BEFORE `run_single_frame` (the emulator stands on that frame)
    end_frame = emulator.get_frame_count()
    end_state = emulator.get_save_state()
    (args.out / "end_state.ss1").write_bytes(end_state)
    marks = story_truth(context)["marks"]
    end_truth = truth(context)
    seen_by_ai = pes.ai_view(FrameworkEyes())
    pages = pes.message_pages()
    behaviour = pes.accepts_input(emulator)
    screen = pes.settled(emulator, args.after)
    data["story"] = recorder.result()
    (args.out / "play.json").write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")

    # ---- THE REACH, read AFTER the run from the loop-top state (nothing ran inside the run)
    reach = None
    if hook["first"] is not None:
        state = states["reach"]
        (args.out / "reach_state.ss1").write_bytes(state)
        emulator.load_save_state(state)
        reach_marks = story_truth(context)["marks"]
        reach_ai = pes.ai_view(FrameworkEyes())
        reach_pages = pes.message_pages()
        reach_input = pes.accepts_input(emulator)
        reach_screen = pes.settled(emulator, args.after)
        reach = {"at": hook["first"], "state_md5": hashlib.md5(state).hexdigest(),
                 "same_bytes_as_end_state": state == end_state, "proof": proof_read(reach_marks, need),
                 "screen": reach_screen, "accepts_input": reach_input, "ai_eyes_for_the_record": reach_ai,
                 "message_pages": reach_pages[:4]}
    (args.out / "hook.json").write_text(json.dumps(hook, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    report = data.get("report") or {}
    outcome_lines = [line for line in data["lines"] if line.startswith("[AI-HANDS] outcome=")]
    return {"seed": args.seed, "target": args.target, "personality": personality, "starter": starter,
            "root": str(root), "frames_budget": args.frames, "start": str(args.state) if args.state else "REAL New Game",
            "reason": data["reason"], "frames": data["frames"], "seconds": round(time.monotonic() - began, 1),
            "stop": ((data.get("game_reads") or {}).get("stop") or {}), "end_emulator_frame": end_frame,
            "play_end": hook["play_end"], "wrapper_calls": hook["calls"], "wrapped": hook["wrapped"],
            "outcome_line": outcome_lines[-1][:200] if outcome_lines else None,
            "ai_end_screen": report.get("end_screen"), "ai_settle": report.get("settle"),
            "end_screen": screen, "accepts_input": behaviour, "ai_eyes_for_the_record": seen_by_ai,
            "end_proof": proof_read(marks, need), "message_pages": pages[:6],
            "end_state": str(args.out / "end_state.ss1"), "end_state_md5": hashlib.md5(end_state).hexdigest(),
            "end_map": end_truth.get("map"), "end_pos": end_truth.get("pos"), "end_game_state": end_truth.get("game_state"),
            "reach": reach, "milestones": [m["id"] for m in report.get("milestones", [])],
            "last_lines": data["lines"][-14:]}


def main() -> int:
    try:
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:
        pass
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("mode", choices=("run",))
    parser.add_argument("--root", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--target", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--frames", type=int, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--after", type=int, default=pes.DEFAULT_AFTER)
    parser.add_argument("--state", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--world-in", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="r5_ab_")))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    result = run(args)
    print("PROBE " + json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==== s53/r5/ab/ab_summary.py (sha256 a6e3118b02847eb354caad6f0d1bacb453cb577c41e2b7e786f67050c72b7f0c, 12257 bytes; LF text sha256 a6e3118b02847eb354caad6f0d1bacb453cb577c41e2b7e786f67050c72b7f0c) ====
"""R5 LIVE A/B: the per-seed table and the VERDICT, by the criteria REGISTERED with the held-out seeds (the ledger entries'
notes and bounds sha256 include this file's sha256), computed from the saved PROBE lines only (never from memory).

    py -3.12 ab_summary.py              -> the table and the verdict of runs/ (save as ab/SUMMARY.txt)
    py -3.12 ab_summary.py --selftest   -> the criteria fault-tested on the instrument check's two replay runs (hookcheck/)

THE CRITERIA (pre-registered):
  * REACH (LESSONS PF-4), per arm: the run decided "done" at a loop top (the first wrapper call, `ab_probe.py`), the GAME's
    proof of the target holds on that loop top's state (`story_truth`), and the harness's HELD free read of that state is
    NOT free (`free_held` False). A seed COUNTS only if BOTH arms reached, at the same loop-top frame (`pad_frames`).
  * REACH FLOOR: 3 counted seeds of the 4 registered A/B seeds. 0 counted -> VACUOUS; 1-2 -> INCOMPLETE.
  * OLD (R5 + G347) on a counted seed: the play loop's own end string is exactly "done: ch2_dad reached", the harness's
    reason exactly "play: done: ch2_dad reached", and the harness's held free read at the END is NOT free.
  * NEW (R5) on a counted seed: the same two exact strings, and the harness's held free read at the END is FREE.
  * PF-17 CONTROL (NEW, target ch1_pokedex, its own held-out seed): the two exact strings for ch1_pokedex, the END held
    free, and the settle played nothing out (`report.settle.played` 0, `report.end_screen.settled` False).
  * VERDICT: FAIL if a counted seed or the control (when it ran) misses its criteria; else VACUOUS / INCOMPLETE by the
    reach floor (or a missing control); else PASS. The behavioural input check and the AI's own `nav_free` are REPORTED
    beside the free read, never instead of it (PF-19).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
AB_TARGET, CONTROL_TARGET = "ch2_dad", "ch1_pokedex"
AB_SEEDS, CONTROL_SEED = (5351, 5352, 5353, 5354), 5355
REACH_FLOOR = 3


def load(folder: Path) -> dict | None:
    out = folder / "probe.out"
    if not out.is_file():
        return None
    text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    line = next((l for l in text.splitlines() if l.startswith("PROBE {")), None)
    exit_line = next((l for l in text.splitlines() if l.startswith("exit=")), None)
    if line is None:
        return {"_missing": True, "_exit": exit_line, "_tail": text[-1500:], "_folder": folder.name}
    data = json.loads(line[len("PROBE "):])
    data["_exit"], data["_folder"] = exit_line, folder.name
    times = [l for l in text.splitlines() if l.startswith(("started", "ended"))]
    data["_times"] = times
    return data


def reached(row: dict | None) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    if row is None or row.get("_missing"):
        return False, "no PROBE line (the run did not finish)"
    reach = row.get("reach")
    if reach is None:
        return False, f"the run never decided 'done' at a loop top (reason {row.get('reason')!r})"
    if not reach["proof"]["holds"]:
        return False, f"the target's proof does not hold at the loop top ({reach['proof']['values']})"
    if reach["screen"]["free_held"]:
        return False, "the screen was HELD FREE at the loop top (nothing to settle: this seed cannot discriminate)"
    return True, (f"proof holds {reach['proof']['values']}; at the loop top free_at_return "
                  f"{reach['screen']['free_at_return']}, free_held False (first_not_free {reach['screen']['first_not_free']})")


def exact(row: dict, target: str) -> bool:
    played = (row.get("play_end") or {}).get("returned")
    return played == f"done: {target} reached" and row.get("reason") == f"play: done: {target} reached"


def old_ok(row: dict, target: str = AB_TARGET) -> bool:
    return exact(row, target) and row["end_screen"]["free_held"] is False


def new_ok(row: dict, target: str = AB_TARGET) -> bool:
    return exact(row, target) and row["end_screen"]["free_held"] is True


def control_ok(row: dict) -> bool:
    settle = row.get("ai_settle") or {}
    end = row.get("ai_end_screen") or {}
    return (exact(row, CONTROL_TARGET) and row["end_screen"]["free_held"] is True and settle.get("played") == 0
            and end.get("settled") is False)


def cells(row: dict | None, arm: str, counted: bool | None) -> str:
    if row is None:
        return f"| {arm} | NOT RUN |"
    if row.get("_missing"):
        return f"| {arm} | NO PROBE LINE ({row.get('_exit')}) |"
    ok, why = reached(row)
    screen, ai_end = row["end_screen"], row.get("ai_end_screen") or {}
    settle = row.get("ai_settle")
    calls = [c["where"] for c in row.get("wrapper_calls", [])]
    reach_at = (row.get("reach") or {}).get("at") or {}
    return (f"| {arm} | reached {ok}{'' if counted is None else (', COUNTED' if counted else ', not counted')} | "
            f"brain {((row.get('play_end') or {}).get('returned'))!r}; harness {row.get('reason')!r} | "
            f"end free_at_return {screen['free_at_return']}, free_held {screen['free_held']} (first_not_free "
            f"{screen['first_not_free']}); input taken {row['accepts_input'].get('turned')} | "
            f"AI nav_free at end: report {ai_end.get('free')}, eyes {row['ai_eyes_for_the_record'].get('nav_free')} | "
            f"settle {settle if settle is not None else 'none (no report.settle)'}; wrapper calls {calls} | "
            f"frames {row['frames']} (loop top at pad frame {reach_at.get('pad_frames')}) |")


def table(base: Path) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
    lines, facts = [], {"counted": [], "not_counted": [], "fails": [], "missing": []}
    for seed in AB_SEEDS:
        old = load(base / f"old_{AB_TARGET}_{seed}")
        new = load(base / f"new_{AB_TARGET}_{seed}")
        old_reach, old_why = reached(old)
        new_reach, new_why = reached(new)
        same_top = (old_reach and new_reach and old["reach"]["at"]["pad_frames"] == new["reach"]["at"]["pad_frames"])
        counted = bool(old_reach and new_reach and same_top)
        head = (f"seed {seed} ({(new or old or {}).get('personality')}/{(new or old or {}).get('starter')}): "
                f"OLD reach: {old_why} | NEW reach: {new_why} | same loop-top frame: {same_top}")
        if old is not None and new is not None and old.get("reach") and new.get("reach"):
            head += (f" | loop-top state md5 OLD {old['reach']['state_md5']} NEW {new['reach']['state_md5']} "
                     f"({'identical' if old['reach']['state_md5'] == new['reach']['state_md5'] else 'DIFFERENT'})")
        lines.append(head)
        for arm, row in (("OLD", old), ("NEW", new)):
            lines.append("  " + cells(row, arm, counted))
        if old is None or new is None or old.get("_missing") or new.get("_missing"):
            facts["missing"].append(seed)
        if counted:
            facts["counted"].append(seed)
            if not old_ok(old):
                facts["fails"].append(f"seed {seed} OLD: expected done (exact) NOT free; got {old['play_end']}, "
                                      f"free_held {old['end_screen']['free_held']}")
            if not new_ok(new):
                facts["fails"].append(f"seed {seed} NEW: expected done (exact) FREE; got {new['play_end']}, "
                                      f"free_held {new['end_screen']['free_held']}")
        else:
            facts["not_counted"].append(seed)
    control = load(base / f"new_{CONTROL_TARGET}_{CONTROL_SEED}")
    lines.append(f"PF-17 CONTROL seed {CONTROL_SEED} ({(control or {}).get('personality')}/{(control or {}).get('starter')}), "
                 f"target {CONTROL_TARGET}, NEW:")
    lines.append("  " + cells(control, "NEW", None))
    if control is None or control.get("_missing"):
        facts["control"] = None
    else:
        facts["control"] = control_ok(control)
        if not facts["control"]:
            facts["fails"].append(f"control seed {CONTROL_SEED}: expected done (exact), FREE, settle played 0; got "
                                  f"{control.get('play_end')}, free_held {control['end_screen']['free_held']}, settle "
                                  f"{control.get('ai_settle')}, end_screen {control.get('ai_end_screen')}")
    return lines, facts


def verdict(facts: dict) -> tuple[str, str]:
    counted = len(facts["counted"])
    if facts["fails"]:
        return "FAIL", "; ".join(facts["fails"])
    if counted == 0:
        return "VACUOUS", f"no seed reached the state in both arms (floor {REACH_FLOOR})"
    if counted < REACH_FLOOR:
        return "INCOMPLETE", f"{counted} counted seed(s), under the reach floor {REACH_FLOOR}"
    if facts.get("control") is None:
        return "INCOMPLETE", "the PF-17 control did not finish"
    return "PASS", (f"{counted} of {len(AB_SEEDS)} seeds counted (floor {REACH_FLOOR}): OLD ended done NOT free and NEW "
                    f"done FREE with the exact reason on every counted seed; the PF-17 control passed")


def selftest() -> int:
    """The criteria must be able to FAIL and PASS (PF-10), on REAL runs: the instrument check's OLD and NEW replays."""
    base = HERE / "hookcheck"
    old, new = load(base / f"old_{AB_TARGET}_5301"), load(base / f"new_{AB_TARGET}_5301")
    if old is None or new is None or old.get("_missing") or new.get("_missing"):
        print("SELFTEST: the hookcheck runs are missing")
        return 1
    cases = []

    def case(name: str, got, want) -> None:
        cases.append((name, got, want, got == want))

    case("OLD replay reached (a not-free loop top)", reached(old)[0], True)
    case("NEW replay reached (the same loop top)", reached(new)[0], True)
    case("the two replays decided done on the same loop-top frame",
         old["reach"]["at"]["pad_frames"] == new["reach"]["at"]["pad_frames"], True)
    case("OLD criteria on OLD", old_ok(old), True)
    case("NEW criteria on NEW", new_ok(new), True)
    case("NEW criteria on OLD (must FAIL: not free at the end)", new_ok(old), False)
    case("OLD criteria on NEW (must FAIL: free at the end)", old_ok(new), False)
    case("control criteria on NEW ch2_dad (must FAIL: another target, and it played the screen out)", control_ok(new), False)
    free_top = copy.deepcopy(new)
    free_top["reach"]["screen"]["free_held"] = True
    case("a loop top HELD FREE is not reached (synthetic)", reached(free_top)[0], False)
    no_proof = copy.deepcopy(new)
    no_proof["reach"]["proof"]["holds"] = False
    case("a loop top without the proof is not reached (synthetic)", reached(no_proof)[0], False)
    stuck = copy.deepcopy(new)
    stuck["play_end"]["returned"] = "stuck: no progress"
    case("NEW criteria with another end string (must FAIL, synthetic)", new_ok(stuck), False)
    prefixed = copy.deepcopy(new)
    prefixed["reason"] = "play: done: ch2_dad reached "
    case("NEW criteria with a near-miss harness reason (must FAIL, synthetic)", new_ok(prefixed), False)
    for name, got, want, ok in cases:
        print(f"  {'ok ' if ok else 'BAD'} {name}: got {got}, want {want}")
    passed = all(ok for *_, ok in cases)
    print(f"SELFTEST VERDICT: {'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL'} ({sum(1 for *_, ok in cases if ok)} of {len(cases)} cases)")
    return 0 if passed else 1


def main() -> int:
    try:
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:
        pass
    if "--selftest" in sys.argv[1:]:
        return selftest()
    lines, facts = table(HERE / "runs")
    print("R5 LIVE A/B (held-out seeds; in-process, REAL New Game, clock pinned). Harness free read at the frame the brain "
          "returned and 10 more frames with no input (free_held = free on all 11) = THE DISCRIMINATOR; 'input taken' = a "
          "tapped direction turned the player in a throwaway copy of that frame; the AI's nav_free is reported beside it.")
    for line in lines:
        print(line)
    result, why = verdict(facts)
    print(f"COUNTED seeds {facts['counted']}; not counted {facts['not_counted']}; missing runs {facts['missing']}; "
          f"control {facts.get('control')}")
    print(f"VERDICT: {result}: {why}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==== s53/r5/ab/ab_table.py (sha256 a76ca77b05b81f7ddd769e130bf3a036bb79fbeed7198c61e773be0767215e75, 3844 bytes; LF text sha256 a76ca77b05b81f7ddd769e130bf3a036bb79fbeed7198c61e773be0767215e75) ====
"""The report's per-seed MARKDOWN table, printed from the saved PROBE lines by the REGISTERED grader's own functions
(`ab_summary.load`, `reached`, `old_ok`, `new_ok`, `control_ok`; that file is not changed). Presentation only: the verdict is
`ab_summary.py`'s (SUMMARY.txt).

    py -3.12 ab_table.py   -> save as ab/table.md
"""

import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import ab_summary as g  # noqa: E402


def fmt(n) -> str:
    return f"{n:,}" if isinstance(n, int) else str(n)


def row(seed: int, arm: str, data: dict | None, counted: str, target: str) -> str:
    if data is None or data.get("_missing"):
        return f"| {seed} | {arm} | (no PROBE line: {None if data is None else data.get('_exit')}) | | | | | | |"
    ok, _ = g.reached(data)
    reach = data.get("reach") or {}
    top = reach.get("at") or {}
    rs = reach.get("screen") or {}
    end = data["end_screen"]
    ai_end = data.get("ai_end_screen") or {}
    settle = data.get("ai_settle")
    played = (data.get("play_end") or {}).get("returned")
    if settle is None:
        ran = "no (no `_settle_before_done` call; G347)" if arm == "OLD" else "no record"
    else:
        ran = (f"played {settle['played']}, paused {settle['paused']}, {fmt(settle['frames'])} frames; settled "
               f"{ai_end.get('settled')}")
    top_text = (f"{ok}: loop top pad frame {fmt(top.get('pad_frames'))} ({top.get('where')}), there free_at_return "
                f"{rs.get('free_at_return')} / free_held {rs.get('free_held')}, proof {reach.get('proof', {}).get('holds')}"
                if reach else f"{ok}: never decided done")
    return (f"| {seed} {data['personality']}/{data['starter']} | {arm} | {top_text}; {counted} | `{played}` / "
            f"`{data.get('reason')}` | free_at_return {end['free_at_return']}, **free_held {end['free_held']}** "
            f"(first_not_free {end['first_not_free']}); input taken {data['accepts_input'].get('turned')} | "
            f"report {ai_end.get('free')}; eyes {data['ai_eyes_for_the_record'].get('nav_free')}"
            + (f"; on its loop-top look {top.get('ai_nav_free_on_its_look')}" if top.get('ai_nav_free_on_its_look') is not None else "")
            + f" | {ran} | {fmt(data['frames'])} |")


def main() -> int:
    try:
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:
        pass
    base = HERE / "runs"
    print("| Seed (personality/starter) | Arm | Reached (PF-4) | End reason: the play loop's / the harness's | Harness free "
          "read at the END (the discriminator) | AI nav_free at the end | Settle ran | Frames |")
    print("|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
    for seed in g.AB_SEEDS:
        old, new = g.load(base / f"old_{g.AB_TARGET}_{seed}"), g.load(base / f"new_{g.AB_TARGET}_{seed}")
        both = g.reached(old)[0] and g.reached(new)[0]
        same = both and old["reach"]["at"]["pad_frames"] == new["reach"]["at"]["pad_frames"]
        counted = "COUNTED" if (both and same) else "not counted"
        for arm, data in (("OLD", old), ("NEW", new)):
            ok = None if data is None or data.get("_missing") else (g.old_ok(data) if arm == "OLD" else g.new_ok(data))
            print(row(seed, arm, data, f"{counted}; arm criteria {'met' if ok else 'NOT met' if ok is False else 'n/a'}",
                      g.AB_TARGET))
    control = g.load(base / f"new_{g.CONTROL_TARGET}_{g.CONTROL_SEED}")
    ok = None if control is None or control.get("_missing") else g.control_ok(control)
    print(row(g.CONTROL_SEED, "NEW (PF-17 control)", control,
              f"a CONTROL, not graded by reach; control criteria {'met' if ok else 'NOT met' if ok is False else 'n/a'}",
              g.CONTROL_TARGET))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==== s53/r5/ab/ab_ledger.py (sha256 f4c52fa75392410a03f5ba953ecf666ceff1354ccb53f423630ea8dfd6f118dd, 8231 bytes; LF text sha256 f4c52fa75392410a03f5ba953ecf666ceff1354ccb53f423630ea8dfd6f118dd) ====
"""R5 live A/B: its seed ranges in the worktree's seed ledger (`ai_player/gates/ledger.py`, the ledger's own functions),
written BEFORE they are played (ai_player/README.md; the ledger module's rules).

    py -3.12 ab_ledger.py --repo WORKTREE numbers  --kind ab|control|hookcheck
    py -3.12 ab_ledger.py --repo WORKTREE register --kind ab|control          (a GRADED range, pre-registered)
    py -3.12 ab_ledger.py --repo WORKTREE begin    --kind ab|control|hookcheck (graded: refused unless registered with the
                                                                                 SAME numbers; hookcheck: a REPLAY of 5301)
    py -3.12 ab_ledger.py --repo WORKTREE finish   --id N --verdict-file F --evidence TEXT

The numbers a range is graded by (`ledger.numbers_sha256`) include the sha256 of the driver, the free read, the grader, the
run script and both arms' tree digests, so a change to any of them between registration and the run is REFUSED by
`ledger.begin`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
from make_trees import digest, files_of  # noqa: E402

KINDS = {
    "ab": {"driver": "r5_ab", "first": 5351, "last": 5354, "target": "ch2_dad", "frames": 400000},
    "control": {"driver": "r5_ab_control", "first": 5355, "last": 5355, "target": "ch1_pokedex", "frames": 300000},
    "hookcheck": {"driver": "r5_ab_hookcheck", "first": 5301, "last": 5301, "target": "ch2_dad", "frames": 120000},
}
REACH = ("REACH (PF-4), per arm: the first wrapper call for a 'done' end (HandsBrain._settle_before_done or "
         "_play_end('done'), rebound by the harness to RECORD only) is the loop top where the run decided done; there the "
         "game's proof of the target holds (play_harness.story_truth) and the harness's HELD free read of that state "
         "(probe_end_screen.settled, 10 more frames, no input) is NOT free. A seed counts only if BOTH arms reached it at the "
         "same loop-top frame.")


def sha(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def numbers(kind: str) -> dict:
    from ai_player.gates.rate_step5 import GAME_CLOCK, WALL_CLOCK

    spec = KINDS[kind]
    trees = {"OLD": digest(files_of(HERE / "trees" / "old_G347" / "ai_player")),
             "NEW": digest(files_of(HERE / "trees" / "new_R5" / "ai_player"))}
    out = {"driver": f"{spec['driver']} (s53/r5/ab/ab_probe.py run via run_ab.sh; in-process play_harness.play)",
           "ab_probe_sha256": sha(HERE / "ab_probe.py"), "free_read_sha256": sha(HERE / "probe_end_screen.py"),
           "grader_sha256": sha(HERE / "ab_summary.py"), "run_script_sha256": sha(HERE / "run_ab.sh"),
           "target": spec["target"], "frames": spec["frames"], "after_frames": 10, "game_clock": GAME_CLOCK,
           "wall_clock": WALL_CLOCK, "seeds": [spec["first"], spec["last"]], "tree_digests": trees,
           "g347_edits_sha256": "2744ad39600840d665a8cbc125903af551590f2a90eab042d13a1314786e3142"}
    if kind == "ab":
        out.update(arms={"OLD": "trees/old_G347: the R5 worktree's ai_player, revert_step5.make_copy with G347's edit (the "
                                "settle's call becomes `pass`)",
                         "NEW": "trees/new_R5: the R5 worktree's ai_player, revert_step5.make_copy with no edit"},
                   reach=REACH, reach_floor=3,
                   expect="OLD: 'done: ch2_dad reached' (the loop's string) and 'play: done: ch2_dad reached' (the "
                          "harness's), END held free read NOT free; NEW: the same two strings, END held free read FREE",
                   start="a REAL New Game (no state)")
    elif kind == "control":
        out.update(arms={"NEW": "trees/new_R5"},
                   expect="PF-17 CONTROL: 'done: ch1_pokedex reached' and 'play: done: ch1_pokedex reached', END held free "
                          "read FREE, report.settle.played 0 and report.end_screen.settled False",
                   start="a REAL New Game (no state)")
    else:
        out.update(arms={"OLD": "trees/old_G347", "NEW": "trees/new_R5"},
                   start="the R5 probe's saved ch2_dad_5301 end state + world.jsonl (s53/r5/build/probe/runs/ch2_dad_5301)",
                   expect="the instrument check: both arms decide done at pad frame 0 on a NOT-free loop top; NEW reproduces "
                          "the builder's devab new_5301 (10,997 frames, settle played 2, paused 112)")
    return out


NOTES = {
    "ab": ("R5 live A/B (build spec section 4; HELD-OUT seeds 5351-5354): target ch2_dad from a REAL New Game, in-process "
           "(play_harness.play), the cartridge clock pinned (rate_step5 GAME_CLOCK), frames 400000, two arms per seed: OLD "

    "control": ("R5 PF-17 CONTROL (build spec section 4; HELD-OUT seed 5355): the normal chapter-1 end, target ch1_pokedex "
                "from a REAL New Game, in-process, the clock pinned, frames 300000, NEW (R5) only, no plant. PASS iff "
                "'done: ch1_pokedex reached' exactly, the END held free read FREE, and the settle played nothing out "
                "(report.settle.played 0, report.end_screen.settled False). Grader s53/r5/ab/ab_summary.py."),
    "hookcheck": ("R5 live A/B INSTRUMENT CHECK, a REPLAY (not a measurement, CLAUDE.md rule 8) of development seed 5301 "
                  "(#43): both arms' frozen trees started from the R5 probe's saved ch2_dad_5301 end state and world "
                  "memory, to check the A/B driver's reach instrument (the loop-top record) and its grader's criteria "
                  "BEFORE any held-out seed is played."),
}


def main() -> int:
    try:
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:
        pass
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--repo", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("mode", choices=("numbers", "register", "begin", "finish"))
    parser.add_argument("--kind", choices=tuple(KINDS))
    parser.add_argument("--id", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--verdict-file", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--evidence")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    sys.path.insert(0, str(args.repo.resolve()))
    from ai_player.gates import ledger
    from ai_player.gates.provenance import line_digest, provenance

    print(f"ledger file: {ledger.LEDGER}")
    if args.mode == "finish":
        verdict = " ".join(args.verdict_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").split())
        ledger.finish(args.id, verdict, args.evidence)
        print(f"finished entry #{args.id}: {verdict}")
        return 0
    spec = KINDS[args.kind]
    found = numbers(args.kind)
    bounds = ledger.numbers_sha256(found)
    print("numbers " + json.dumps(found, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True))
    print(f"numbers sha256 {bounds}")
    if args.mode == "numbers":
        return 0
    if args.mode == "register":
        entry = ledger.register("story", spec["driver"], spec["first"], spec["last"], bounds, note=NOTES[args.kind])
        print("REGISTERED " + json.dumps(entry, ensure_ascii=False))
        return 0
    purpose = "replay" if args.kind == "hookcheck" else "graded"
    code = provenance(args.repo.resolve())
    print("code: " + code)
    entry, lines = ledger.begin("story", spec["driver"], purpose, spec["first"], spec["last"], bounds, line_digest(code),
                                note=NOTES[args.kind])
    print("\n".join(lines))
    print("ENTRY " + json.dumps(entry, ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==== s53/r5/ab/make_trees.py (sha256 09b49343d1df1191764895d48e215ac99823219f044219f6cf13cae19f45da2e, 4490 bytes; LF text sha256 09b49343d1df1191764895d48e215ac99823219f044219f6cf13cae19f45da2e) ====
"""R5 live A/B: build the two arms' FROZEN trees with revert_step5's OWN `make_copy` (TEST SIDE).

    py -3.12 make_trees.py --worktree WORKTREE      (prints the record; save it as make_trees.out)

OLD = the R5 worktree's `ai_player/` with mutant G347 applied (the settle's call becomes `pass`), NEW = the same copy with no
edit (revert_step5's G0 control). Both are made by `revert_step5.make_copy` itself (copytree ignoring `__pycache__`,
`evidence`, `*.ss1`; each anchor must occur exactly once), with `tempfile.tempdir` pointed at this folder's `trees/` so the
copies land here and never in the worktree. Then: each copy's digest, the files where OLD and NEW differ (must be ONLY
`mode.py`, by exactly G347's edit), and the NEW copy compared file by file against the worktree's `ai_player/` (must be
identical, the ignored patterns aside).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import difflib
import hashlib
import shutil
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
IGNORED_PARTS = {"__pycache__", "evidence"}


def files_of(root: Path) -> dict[str, bytes]:
    out = {}
    for path in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        rel = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        parts = set(rel.split("/"))
        if parts & IGNORED_PARTS or rel.endswith(".ss1"):
            continue
        out[rel] = path.read_bytes()
    return out


def digest(files: dict[str, bytes]) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for rel in sorted(files):
        h.update(rel.encode("utf-8") + b"\0" + hashlib.sha256(files[rel]).digest())
    return h.hexdigest()


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--worktree", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    worktree = args.worktree.resolve()
    trees = HERE / "trees"
    trees.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    for name in ("old_G347", "new_R5"):
        if (trees / name).exists():
            raise SystemExit(f"{trees / name} exists already: refusing to overwrite a frozen tree")
    tempfile.tempdir = str(trees)
    sys.path.insert(0, str(worktree))
    from ai_player.gates import revert_step5

    print(f"revert_step5 module: {revert_step5.__file__}")
    print(f"revert_step5.REPO: {revert_step5.REPO}")
    prediction, rule, edits, tests = revert_step5.MUTANTS["G347"]
    print(f"G347: {prediction}: {rule}")
    for relative, old, new in edits:
        print(f"  edit in {relative}:\n    OLD TEXT {old!r}\n    NEW TEXT {new!r}")
    print(f"  predicted to fail: {tests}")
    edit_sha = hashlib.sha256(repr(edits).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    print(f"  G347 edits sha256 (repr): {edit_sha}")
    old_root = revert_step5.make_copy(edits)
    new_root = revert_step5.make_copy([])
    old_final, new_final = trees / "old_G347", trees / "new_R5"
    shutil.move(str(old_root), str(old_final))
    shutil.move(str(new_root), str(new_final))
    print(f"OLD tree: {old_final} (made by make_copy as {old_root.name})")
    print(f"NEW tree: {new_final} (made by make_copy as {new_root.name})")
    old_files, new_files = files_of(old_final / "ai_player"), files_of(new_final / "ai_player")
    work_files = files_of(worktree / "ai_player")
    print(f"OLD ai_player: {len(old_files)} files, digest {digest(old_files)}")
    print(f"NEW ai_player: {len(new_files)} files, digest {digest(new_files)}")
    print(f"worktree ai_player (same ignores): {len(work_files)} files, digest {digest(work_files)}")
    differ = sorted(rel for rel in set(old_files) | set(new_files) if old_files.get(rel) != new_files.get(rel))
    print(f"files where OLD and NEW differ: {differ}")
    for rel in differ:
        a = new_files.get(rel, b"").decode("utf-8", "replace").splitlines()
        b = old_files.get(rel, b"").decode("utf-8", "replace").splitlines()
        for row in difflib.unified_diff(a, b, f"NEW/{rel}", f"OLD/{rel}", n=2, lineterm=""):
            print("  " + row)
    vs_work = sorted(rel for rel in set(new_files) | set(work_files) if new_files.get(rel) != work_files.get(rel))
    print(f"files where NEW and the worktree differ: {vs_work if vs_work else 'NONE (identical)'}")
    ok = differ == ["mode.py"] and not vs_work
    print(f"TREES {'OK' if ok else 'NOT OK'}: OLD differs from NEW only in mode.py: {differ == ['mode.py']}; NEW identical "
          f"to the worktree: {not vs_work}")
    return 0 if ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==== s53/r5/ab/run_ab.sh (sha256 ae10b967527fa6a80e04342fd8e757f69f0144dc2e3e96ce50850f4af153d99f, 1777 bytes; LF text sha256 ae10b967527fa6a80e04342fd8e757f69f0144dc2e3e96ce50850f4af153d99f) ====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# One R5 live A/B run: run_ab.sh GROUP ARM SEED TARGET FRAMES [STATE WORLD]
#   GROUP  the folder under ab/ (runs = the held-out A/B and control; hookcheck = the instrument check)
#   ARM    old (trees/old_G347: R5 with mutant G347, the settle's call deleted) or new (trees/new_R5: R5)
#   STATE/WORLD (hookcheck only) a saved state and world memory to start from instead of a REAL New Game
# Output: ab/GROUP/<arm>_<target>_<seed>/probe.out (the PROBE line, the exit code, the 12-hour LA start/end times).
AB="C:/Users/Ripto/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Remote-Projects-Pokebot-Gen3-Enhancement/2cb1657d-9e66-4a34-afd4-cbf84eb4a4a8/scratchpad/s53/r5/ab"
W="C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement/.claude/worktrees/wf_ab039e6b-79e-1"
GROUP="$1"; ARM="$2"; SEED="$3"; TARGET="$4"; FRAMES="$5"; STATE="$6"; WORLD="$7"
case "$ARM" in
  old) TREE="$AB/trees/old_G347" ;;
  new) TREE="$AB/trees/new_R5" ;;
  *) echo "ARM must be old or new"; exit 9 ;;
esac
OUT="$AB/$GROUP/${ARM}_${TARGET}_${SEED}"
if [ -e "$OUT/probe.out" ]; then echo "$OUT/probe.out exists: refusing to play a seed twice into one folder"; exit 9; fi
mkdir -p "$OUT"
cd "$AB" || exit 9
EXTRA=()
if [ -n "$STATE" ]; then EXTRA=(--state "$STATE" --world-in "$WORLD"); fi
for v in $(env | grep -o '^AI_PLAYER_[A-Z0-9_]*'); do unset "$v"; done
{
  date "+started %I:%M:%S %p (LA)"
  echo "group=$GROUP arm=$ARM tree=$TREE seed=$SEED target=$TARGET frames=$FRAMES start=${STATE:-REAL New Game}"
  AI_PLAYER_BUILD_ROOT="$W/sandbox-pokebot" PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 timeout 7200 \
    py -3.12 ab_probe.py run --root "$TREE" --seed "$SEED" --target "$TARGET" --frames "$FRAMES" --out "$OUT" "${EXTRA[@]}"
  echo "exit=$?"
  date "+ended %I:%M:%S %p (LA)"
} > "$OUT/probe.out" 2>&1

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# R5 live A/B, after every run ended: finalize.sh summary   -> SUMMARY.txt (the grader's table + verdict) and table.md
#                                     finalize.sh ledger    -> finish #45/#46 from verdict_45.txt/verdict_46.txt, then

AB="C:/Users/Ripto/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Remote-Projects-Pokebot-Gen3-Enhancement/2cb1657d-9e66-4a34-afd4-cbf84eb4a4a8/scratchpad/s53/r5/ab"

  py -3.12 ab_table.py > table.md 2>&1; echo "table exit=$?" >> table.md
  cat SUMMARY.txt
elif [ "$1" = "ledger" ]; then

    { date "+%I:%M:%S %p (LA)"; py -3.12 ab_ledger.py --repo "$W" finish --id "$ID" --verdict-file "verdict_$ID.txt" \
        --evidence "session scratch s53/r5/ab/AB-REPORT.md (SUMMARY.txt; runs/*/probe.out)"; echo "exit=$?"; } > "ledger_finish_$ID.out" 2>&1
    cat "ledger_finish_$ID.out"

  cat ledger_check_final.out
else
  echo "usage: finalize.sh summary|ledger"; exit 2
fi

==== s53/r5/ab/heldout_check.py (sha256 5b8e249e86206168d01a63477680116a7863ecc62f541e6a6763f65cbba7ed06, 1192 bytes; LF text sha256 5b8e249e86206168d01a63477680116a7863ecc62f541e6a6763f65cbba7ed06) ====
"""Which seed-ledger entries, in EVERY ledger.json under the main checkout and its worktrees, overlap seeds 5351-5355
(this A/B's held-out seeds)? Ours (#45, #46, written by this A/B) are listed separately.

    py -3.12 heldout_check.py   -> save as ab/heldout_check.out
"""

import json
from pathlib import Path

MAIN = Path("C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement")
FIRST, LAST = 5351, 5355
ledgers = [MAIN / "ai_player" / "gates" / "ledger.json"] + sorted(
    (MAIN / ".claude" / "worktrees").glob("*/ai_player/gates/ledger.json"))
for path in ledgers:
    entries = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("entries", [])
    hits = [e for e in entries if e["first"] <= LAST and FIRST <= e["last"]]
    ours = [e["id"] for e in hits if e.get("driver") in ("r5_ab", "r5_ab_control")]
    others = [(e["id"], e["driver"], e["purpose"], e["first"], e["last"]) for e in hits
              if e.get("driver") not in ("r5_ab", "r5_ab_control")]
    top = max((e["last"] for e in entries), default=None)
    print(f"{path}: {len(entries)} entries, highest seed named {top}; overlapping {FIRST}-{LAST}: "
          f"this A/B's own {ours or 'none'}, any other {others or 'NONE'}")

==== s53/r5/ab/tutorial_check.py (sha256 0b256845e9b70eb7622eb02c9404c1f5546b36b65054637a92fd4acb56039773, 1874 bytes; LF text sha256 0b256845e9b70eb7622eb02c9404c1f5546b36b65054637a92fd4acb56039773) ====
"""After each NEW run's loop top, does its action log show WALLY's catching tutorial (the battle where the player's party
leads with WALLY's loaned ZIGZAGOON, `special LoadWallyZigzagoon`, against a wild RALTS)? And what does each OLD log show
after its loop top (nothing: it ended there)? Read from the saved action logs only.

    py -3.12 tutorial_check.py   -> save as ab/tutorial_check.txt
"""

import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
TOPS = {5351: 226470, 5352: 252159, 5353: 196357, 5354: 309762}
for seed, top in TOPS.items():
    for arm in ("old", "new"):
        log = HERE / "runs" / f"{arm}_ch2_dad_{seed}" / "run" / "actions.jsonl"
        after = []
        for line in log.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            try:
                row = json.loads(line)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            frame = row.get("frame")
            if isinstance(frame, int) and frame > top:
                after.append(row)
        battles = [r for r in after if (r.get("digest") or "").find(" battle ") >= 0]
        tutorial = [r for r in battles if re.search(r"party=\[Zigzagoon:L7 ", r.get("digest") or "")
                    and "foe=[Ralts" in (r.get("digest") or "")]
        maps = []
        for r in after:
            found = re.search(r"f\d+ \S+ (?:ui=\S+ )?(?:waits=\S+ \S+ \S+ )?([A-Z][A-Z .']+)\(", r.get("digest") or "")
            if found and (not maps or maps[-1] != found.group(1).strip()):
                maps.append(found.group(1).strip())
        first = tutorial[0]["digest"][:150] if tutorial else None
        print(f"{arm}_{seed}: {len(after)} action rows after the loop top (frame {top}); battle rows {len(battles)}; rows "
              f"with WALLY's Zigzagoon L7 leading against a RALTS {len(tutorial)}; first: {first}; maps in order: {maps[:8]}")

==== s53/r5/ab/ledger_rows.py (sha256 02ee9a8a089b094e626d2926077e35328e9c3d27a23b9566e9cbeba5d7352afa, 578 bytes; LF text sha256 02ee9a8a089b094e626d2926077e35328e9c3d27a23b9566e9cbeba5d7352afa) ====
"""Print the seed-ledger rows this A/B wrote (#44-#46), VERBATIM from the worktree's ledger.json (one JSON line each).

    py -3.12 ledger_rows.py WORKTREE   -> save as ab/ledger_rows.txt
"""

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ledger = Path(sys.argv[1]) / "ai_player" / "gates" / "ledger.json"
print(f"ledger file: {ledger}")
for raw in ledger.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
    text = raw.strip().rstrip(",")
    if not text.startswith("{\"id\""):
        continue
    entry = json.loads(text)
    if entry["id"] in (44, 45, 46):
        print(text)
